为什么状态编辑很重要。Why the state edit matters.重要的行为是延迟修正。该政策的早期假设称The important behavior is the delayed correction. The policy’s early hypothesis says恶霸Bully可能是错误的；尽管如此，安全带仍将其保持在较低的重要性。当稍后的读取与假设相矛盾时，策略可以编辑策划集，保留更正的实体，丢弃过时的变体，并将搜索重定向到发布日期方面。因此，回溯可视为外部状态的变化，而不仅仅是两个相邻搜索之间的私人想法。is probably wrong; the harness nevertheless preserves it at low importance. When a later read contradicts the hypothesis, the policy can edit the curated set, keep the corrected entity, discard stale variants, and redirect search toward the release-date facet. Backtracking is therefore visible as a change to the external state, not just a private thought between two adjacent searches.

56

NeurIPS 论文清单NeurIPS Paper Checklist

1. 索赔Claims

问题：摘要和引言中提出的主要主张是否准确反映了论文的贡献和范围？Question: Do the main claims made in the abstract and introduction accurately reflect the paper’s contributions and scope?

答案：[是]Answer: [Yes]

理由：摘要和引言陈述了本文作为有状态检索工具和 SFT/RL 配方的贡献，结果部分报告了八个基准的实证范围。限制段落限定了权利要求的范围。Justification: The abstract and introduction state the paper’s contribution as a stateful retrieval harness plus SFT/RL recipe, and the results section reports the empirical scope across eight benchmarks. The limitations paragraph qualifies the scope of the claims.

指南：Guidelines:

  • 答案[N/A]意味着摘要和引言不包括论文中提出的权利要求。The answer [N/A] means that the abstract and introduction do not include the claims made in the paper.

  • 摘要和/或引言应清楚地说明所提出的主张，包括论文中所做的贡献以及重要的假设和限制。审稿人不会很好地理解此问题的[否]或[不适用]答案。The abstract and/or introduction should clearly state the claims made, including the contributions made in the paper and important assumptions and limitations. A [No] or [N/A] answer to this question will not be perceived well by the reviewers.

  • 所提出的主张应与理论和实验结果相匹配，并反映结果可以在多大程度上推广到其他设置。The claims made should match theoretical and experimental results, and reflect how much the results can be expected to generalize to other settings.

  • 只要论文明确没有实现这些目标，就可以将理想目标作为动机。It is fine to include aspirational goals as motivation as long as it is clear that these goals are not attained by the paper.

2. 局限性Limitations

问题：本文是否讨论了作者所做工作的局限性？答案：[是]Question: Does the paper discuss the limitations of the work performed by the authors? Answer: [Yes]

理由：附录 B 讨论了限制，包括任务范围、基准测试风格、基于正则表达式的证据图、基于 LLM 的验证器、句子 BM25 压缩、基准测试大小和注释覆盖率。Justification: Appendix B discusses limitations including the task scope, benchmark style, regex-based evidence graph, LLM-based verifier, sentence-BM25 compression, benchmark size, and annotation coverage.

指南：Guidelines:

  • 答案[N/A]意味着论文没有限制，而答案[否]意味着论文有局限性，但论文中没有讨论这些局限性。The answer [N/A] means that the paper has no limitation while the answer [No] means that the paper has limitations, but those are not discussed in the paper.

  • 鼓励作者在论文中创建一个单独的“局限性”部分。The authors are encouraged to create a separate “Limitations” section in their paper.

  • 论文应指出任何强有力的假设以及结果对违反这些假设的鲁棒性如何（例如，独立假设、无噪声设置、模型良好规范、仅在局部保持的渐近近似）。作者应该反思这些假设在实践中可能如何被违反以及会产生什么影响。The paper should point out any strong assumptions and how robust the results are to violations of these assumptions (e.g., independence assumptions, noiseless settings, model well-specification, asymptotic approximations only holding locally). The authors should reflect on how these assumptions might be violated in practice and what the implications would be.

  • 作者应该反思所提出的主张的范围，例如，如果该方法仅在几个数据集或几次运行中进行了测试。一般来说，实证结果通常取决于隐含的假设，这些假设应该被阐明。The authors should reflect on the scope of the claims made, e.g., if the approach was only tested on a few datasets or with a few runs. In general, empirical results often depend on implicit assumptions, which should be articulated.

  • 作者应该反思影响该方法性能的因素。例如，当图像分辨率较低或在低光照条件下拍摄图像时，面部识别算法可能表现不佳。或者，语音转文本系统可能无法可靠地用于为在线讲座提供隐藏式字幕，因为它无法处理技术术语。The authors should reflect on the factors that influence the performance of the approach. For example, a facial recognition algorithm may perform poorly when image resolution is low or images are taken in low lighting. Or a speech-to-text system might not be used reliably to provide closed captions for online lectures because it fails to handle technical jargon.

  • 作者应该讨论所提出算法的计算效率以及它们如何随着数据集大小进行扩展。The authors should discuss the computational efficiency of the proposed algorithms and how they scale with dataset size.

  • 如果适用，作者应该讨论他们解决隐私和公平问题的方法可能存在的局限性。If applicable, the authors should discuss possible limitations of their approach to address problems of privacy and fairness.

  • 虽然作者可能担心审稿人可能会利用对限制的完全诚实作为拒绝的理由，但更糟糕的结果可能是审稿人发现论文中未承认的限制。作者应该运用他们的最佳判断，并认识到有利于透明度的个人行动在制定维护社区完整性的规范方面发挥着重要作用。将特别指示审稿人不要惩罚有关限制的诚实行为。While the authors might fear that complete honesty about limitations might be used by reviewers as grounds for rejection, a worse outcome might be that reviewers discover limitations that aren’t acknowledged in the paper. The authors should use their best judgment and recognize that individual actions in favor of transparency play an important role in developing norms that preserve the integrity of the community. Reviewers will be specifically instructed to not penalize honesty concerning limitations.

  • 理论假设和证明Theory assumptions and proofs

57

问题：对于每个理论结果，论文是否提供了全套假设和完整（且正确）的证明？Question: For each theoretical result, does the paper provide the full set of assumptions and a complete (and correct) proof?

答案：[不适用]Answer: [N/A]

理由：本文是实证性的，不提供理论结果、定理或证明。形式符号用于定义代理接口和利用状态，而不是做出定理级别的声明。Justification: The paper is empirical and does not present theoretical results, theorems, or proofs. The formal notation is used to define the agent interface and harness state, not to make a theorem-level claim.

指南：Guidelines:

  • 答案 [N/A] 意味着该论文不包含理论结果。The answer [N/A] means that the paper does not include theoretical results.

  • 论文中的所有定理、公式和证明都应编号并交叉引用。All the theorems, formulas, and proofs in the paper should be numbered and crossreferenced.

  • 所有假设都应在任何定理的陈述中清楚地陈述或引用。All assumptions should be clearly stated or referenced in the statement of any theorems.

  • 证明可以出现在主论文或补充材料中，但如果它们出现在补充材料中，则鼓励作者提供简短的证明草图以提供直觉。The proofs can either appear in the main paper or the supplemental material, but if they appear in the supplemental material, the authors are encouraged to provide a short proof sketch to provide intuition.

  • 相反，论文核心中提供的任何非正式证明都应由附录或补充材料中提供的正式证明进行补充。Inversely, any informal proof provided in the core of the paper should be complemented by formal proofs provided in appendix or supplemental material.

  • 应正确引用证明所依赖的定理和引理。Theorems and Lemmas that the proof relies upon should be properly referenced.

4. 实验结果重现性Experimental result reproducibility

问：论文是否充分披露了重现论文主要实验结果所需的所有信息，并达到影响论文主要主张和/或结论的程度（无论是否提供代码和数据）？Question: Does the paper fully disclose all the information needed to reproduce the main experimental results of the paper to the extent that it affects the main claims and/or conclusions of the paper (regardless of whether the code and data are provided or not)?

答案：[是]Answer: [Yes]

理由：正文和附录中详细说明了方法、利用算法、奖励定义、超参数、基准统计、提示和评估配方。这些细节涵盖了影响论文主要实验主张的组成部分。指南：Justification: The method, harness algorithms, reward definition, hyperparameters, benchmark statistics, prompts, and evaluation recipe are specified in the main text and appendix. These details cover the components that affect the paper’s main experimental claims. Guidelines:

  • 答案 [N/A] 意味着该论文不包含实验。The answer [N/A] means that the paper does not include experiments.

  • 如果论文包含实验，审稿人不会很好地理解这个问题的“否”答案：无论是否提供代码和数据，使论文具有可重复性很重要。If the paper includes experiments, a [No] answer to this question will not be perceived well by the reviewers: Making the paper reproducible is important, regardless of whether the code and data are provided or not.

  • 如果贡献的是数据集和/或模型，作者应描述为使其结果可重现或可验证而采取的步骤。If the contribution is a dataset and/or model, the authors should describe the steps taken to make their results reproducible or verifiable.

  • 根据贡献，可以通过多种方式实现可重复性。例如，如果贡献是一种新颖的架构，充分描述该架构可能就足够了，或者如果贡献是一个特定的模型和实证评估，则可能有必要让其他人能够使用相同的数据集复制该模型，或者提供对该模型的访问。一般来说。发布代码和数据通常是实现这一目标的一种好方法，但也可以通过详细说明如何复制结果、访问托管模型（例如，在大型语言模型的情况下）、发布模型检查点或适合所执行的研究的其他方式来提供可重复性。Depending on the contribution, reproducibility can be accomplished in various ways. For example, if the contribution is a novel architecture, describing the architecture fully might suffice, or if the contribution is a specific model and empirical evaluation, it may be necessary to either make it possible for others to replicate the model with the same dataset, or provide access to the model. In general. releasing code and data is often one good way to accomplish this, but reproducibility can also be provided via detailed instructions for how to replicate the results, access to a hosted model (e.g., in the case of a large language model), releasing of a model checkpoint, or other means that are appropriate to the research performed.

  • 虽然 NeurIPS 不要求发布代码，但会议确实要求所有提交内容提供一些合理的可重复性途径，这可能取决于贡献的性质。例如While NeurIPS does not require releasing code, the conference does require all submissions to provide some reasonable avenue for reproducibility, which may depend on the nature of the contribution. For example

  • (a) 如果贡献主要是一个新算法，论文应该明确如何重现该算法。(a) If the contribution is primarily a new algorithm, the paper should make it clear how to reproduce that algorithm.

  • (b) 如果贡献主要是一个新的模型架构，论文应该清晰、完整地描述该架构。(b) If the contribution is primarily a new model architecture, the paper should describe the architecture clearly and fully.

  • (c) 如果贡献的是一个新模型（例如，一个大型语言模型），那么应该有一种方法来访问该模型以重现结果，或者一种重现模型的方法（例如，使用开源数据集或如何构建数据集的说明）。(c) If the contribution is a new model (e.g., a large language model), then there should either be a way to access this model for reproducing the results or a way to reproduce the model (e.g., with an open-source dataset or instructions for how to construct the dataset).

58

  • (d) 我们认识到，在某些情况下，可重复性可能很棘手，在这种情况下，欢迎作者描述他们提供可重复性的特定方式。在闭源模型的情况下，对模型的访问可能会受到某种方式的限制（例如，注册用户），但其他研究人员应该有可能有某种途径来复制或验证结果。(d) We recognize that reproducibility may be tricky in some cases, in which case authors are welcome to describe the particular way they provide for reproducibility. In the case of closed-source models, it may be that access to the model is limited in some way (e.g., to registered users), but it should be possible for other researchers to have some path to reproducing or verifying the results.

5. 开放数据和代码的访问Open access to data and code

问题：论文是否提供对数据和代码的开放访问，并提供足够的说明来忠实地再现主要实验结果，如补充材料中所述？Question: Does the paper provide open access to the data and code, with sufficient instructions to faithfully reproduce the main experimental results, as described in supplemental material?

答案：[否]Answer: [No]

理由：该论文提出了发布权重、利用代码、数据生成管道和 RL 配方的计划，但提交内容本身尚未提供对所有资产的开放公共访问。尽管如此，附录仍包含实验方案的重现性详细信息。Justification: The paper states a plan to release weights, harness code, data-generation pipeline, and RL recipe, but the submission itself does not yet provide open public access to all assets. The appendix nevertheless includes reproducibility details for the experimental protocol.

指南：Guidelines:

  • 答案 [N/A] 意味着论文不包含需要代码的实验。The answer [N/A] means that paper does not include experiments requiring code.

  • 请参阅 NeurIPS 代码和数据提交指南（Please see the NeurIPS code and data submission guidelines (https://neurips. cc/public/guides/CodeSubmissionPolicy）了解更多详情。) for more details.

  • 虽然我们鼓励发布代码和数据，但我们知道这可能是不可能的，因此[否]是一个可以接受的答案。论文不能仅仅因为不包含代码而被拒绝，除非这是贡献的核心（例如，对于新的开源基准）。While we encourage the release of code and data, we understand that this might not be possible, so [No] is an acceptable answer. Papers cannot be rejected simply for not including code, unless this is central to the contribution (e.g., for a new open-source benchmark).

  • 说明应包含运行以重现结果所需的确切命令和环境。请参阅 NeurIPS 代码和数据提交指南（The instructions should contain the exact command and environment needed to run to reproduce the results. See the NeurIPS code and data submission guidelines (https: //neurips.cc/public/guides/CodeSubmissionPolicy）了解更多详情。) for more details.

  • 作者应提供数据访问和准备的说明，包括如何访问原始数据、预处理数据、中间数据和生成数据等。The authors should provide instructions on data access and preparation, including how to access the raw data, preprocessed data, intermediate data, and generated data, etc.

  • 作者应提供脚本来重现新提出的方法和基线的所有实验结果。如果只有一部分实验是可重复的，他们应该说明脚本中省略了哪些实验以及原因。The authors should provide scripts to reproduce all experimental results for the new proposed method and baselines. If only a subset of experiments are reproducible, they should state which ones are omitted from the script and why.

  • 在提交时，为了保持匿名，作者应发布匿名版本（如果适用）。At submission time, to preserve anonymity, the authors should release anonymized versions (if applicable).

  • 建议在补充材料（附加到论文中）中提供尽可能多的信息，但允许包含数据和代码的 URL。Providing as much information as possible in supplemental material (appended to the paper) is recommended, but including URLs to data and code is permitted.

6. 实验设置/细节Experimental setting/details

问题：论文是否指定了理解结果所需的所有训练和测试细节（例如，数据分割、超参数、如何选择它们、优化器的类型）？答案：[是]Question: Does the paper specify all the training and test details (e.g., data splits, hyperparameters, how they were chosen, type of optimizer) necessary to understand the results? Answer: [Yes]

理由：实验设置总结了基准系列、源和传输分割解释、基线和指标。附录表提供了基准统计数据、训练和评估超参数、奖励权重、提示和评估配方。Justification: The experimental setup summarizes the benchmark families, source and transfer split interpretation, baselines, and metrics. Appendix tables provide benchmark statistics, training and evaluation hyperparameters, reward weights, prompts, and the evaluation recipe.

指南：Guidelines:

  • 答案 [N/A] 意味着该论文不包含实验。The answer [N/A] means that the paper does not include experiments.

  • 实验设置应在论文的核心部分详细介绍，以便欣赏结果并理解结果。The experimental setting should be presented in the core of the paper to a level of detail that is necessary to appreciate the results and make sense of them.

  • 完整的详细信息可以在代码中、附录中或作为补充材料提供。The full details can be provided either with the code, in appendix, or as supplemental material.

7. 实验统计显着性Experiment statistical significance

问题：论文是否报告了适当且正确定义的误差线或有关实验统计显着性的其他适当信息？Question: Does the paper report error bars suitably and correctly defined or other appropriate information about the statistical significance of the experiments?

答案：[否]Answer: [No]

59

理由：本文报告了固定基准测试集的聚合点估计，并在表 7 中给出了每个基准的评估实例数。我们没有报告所有主要比较的正式置信区间或显着性测试。这是当前提交的限制：重复运行评估将需要许多额外的多回合代理部署，包括跨多个系统和基准的 Tinker 推理调用和外部检索/重新排名调用，这在计算和财务上都很昂贵。我们在限制部分讨论由此产生的不确定性，特别是对于较小的基准。Justification: The paper reports aggregate point estimates on fixed benchmark test sets and gives the number of evaluated instances for each benchmark in Table 7. We do not report formal confidence intervals or significance tests for all main comparisons. This is a limitation of the current submission: repeated-run evaluation would require many additional multi-turn agent rollouts, including Tinker inference calls and external retrieval/reranking calls across several systems and benchmarks, which is computationally and financially expensive. We discuss the resulting uncertainty, especially for smaller benchmarks, in the limitations section.

指南：Guidelines:

  • 答案 [N/A] 意味着该论文不包含实验。The answer [N/A] means that the paper does not include experiments.

  • 如果结果附有误差线、置信区间或统计显着性检验，至少对于支持论文主要主张的实验，作者应该回答“是”。The authors should answer [Yes] if the results are accompanied by error bars, confidence intervals, or statistical significance tests, at least for the experiments that support the main claims of the paper.

  • 应明确说明误差线捕获的变异因素（例如，训练/测试分割、初始化、某些参数的随机绘制或给定实验条件下的总体运行）。The factors of variability that the error bars are capturing should be clearly stated (for example, train/test split, initialization, random drawing of some parameter, or overall run with given experimental conditions).

  • 应解释计算误差线的方法（封闭式公式、调用库函数、引导程序等）The method for calculating the error bars should be explained (closed form formula, call to a library function, bootstrap, etc.)

  • 应给出所做的假设（例如，正态分布误差）。The assumptions made should be given (e.g., Normally distributed errors).

  • 应明确误差条是标准差还是平均值的标准误差。It should be clear whether the error bar is the standard deviation or the standard error of the mean.

  • 报告 1-sigma 误差线是可以的，但应该说明这一点。如果错误正态性假设未得到验证，作者最好报告 2-sigma 误差线，而不是声明其 CI 为 96%。It is OK to report 1-sigma error bars, but one should state it. The authors should preferably report a 2-sigma error bar than state that they have a 96% CI, if the hypothesis of Normality of errors is not verified.

  • 对于不对称分布，作者应注意不要在表格或图中显示对称误差条，否则会产生超出范围的结果（例如负误差率）。For asymmetric distributions, the authors should be careful not to show in tables or figures symmetric error bars that would yield results that are out of range (e.g., negative error rates).

  • 如果在表格或图中报告了误差线，作者应在文本中解释它们是如何计算的，并在文本中引用相应的图形或表格。If error bars are reported in tables or plots, the authors should explain in the text how they were calculated and reference the corresponding figures or tables in the text.

8. 实验计算资源Experiments compute resources

问题：对于每个实验，论文是否提供了重现实验所需的计算机资源（计算工作者类型、内存、执行时间）的足够信息？Question: For each experiment, does the paper provide sufficient information on the computer resources (type of compute workers, memory, time of execution) needed to reproduce the experiments?

答案：[是]Answer: [Yes]

理由：论文报告了训练平台、模型、SFT 和 RL 数据规模、部署预算、训练步骤数、推理设置、检索后端、重新排序器和评估协议。培训是使用 Tinker（一项托管培训服务）进行的；因此，确切的低级工作线程配置（例如物理工作线程类型和内存分配）由平台抽象。我们报告了重现实验所需的可用的计算相关设置。Justification: The paper reports the training platform, model, SFT and RL data scale, rollout budget, number of training steps, inference settings, retrieval backend, reranker, and evaluation protocol. Training was conducted using Tinker, a managed training service; therefore, exact low-level worker configurations such as physical worker type and memory allocation are abstracted by the platform. We report the available compute-relevant settings needed to reproduce the experiments.

指南：Guidelines:

  • 答案 [N/A] 意味着该论文不包含实验。The answer [N/A] means that the paper does not include experiments.

  • 论文应指出计算工作人员 CPU 或 GPU、内部集群或云提供商的类型，包括相关内存和存储。The paper should indicate the type of compute workers CPU or GPU, internal cluster, or cloud provider, including relevant memory and storage.

  • 论文应提供每次实验运行所需的计算量并估计总计算量。The paper should provide the amount of compute required for each of the individual experimental runs as well as estimate the total compute.

  • 论文应披露整个研究项目是否需要比论文中报告的实验更多的计算（例如，未纳入论文的初步或失败的实验）。The paper should disclose whether the full research project required more compute than the experiments reported in the paper (e.g., preliminary or failed experiments that didn’t make it into the paper).

9. 道德准则Code of ethics

问题：论文中进行的研究是否在各个方面都符合 NeurIPS 道德准则Question: Does the research conducted in the paper conform, in every respect, with the NeurIPS Code of Ethicshttps://neurips.cc/public/EthicsGuidelines ?

答案：[是]Answer: [Yes]

60

理由：据我们所知，这项工作符合 NeurIPS 道德准则。它使用基准和公共领域或引用的语料库来源，不涉及欺骗或人体实验，并保留提交的匿名性。指南：Justification: To the best of our knowledge, the work conforms to the NeurIPS Code of Ethics. It uses benchmark and public-domain or cited corpus sources, does not involve deception or human-subject experiments, and preserves submission anonymity. Guidelines:

  • 答案 [N/A] 意味着作者尚未审查 NeurIPS 道德准则。The answer [N/A] means that the authors have not reviewed the NeurIPS Code of Ethics.

  • 如果作者回答[否]，他们应该解释需要偏离道德准则的特殊情况。If the authors answer [No], they should explain the special circumstances that require a deviation from the Code of Ethics.

  • 作者应确保保持匿名（例如，如果由于其管辖范围内的法律或法规而有特殊考虑）。The authors should make sure to preserve anonymity (e.g., if there is a special consideration due to laws or regulations in their jurisdiction).

10. 更广泛的影响Broader impacts

问题：本文是否讨论了所开展工作的潜在积极社会影响和消极社会影响？Question: Does the paper discuss both potential positive societal impacts and negative societal impacts of the work performed?

答案：[是]Answer: [Yes]

理由：更广泛的影响和发布段落讨论了低成本证据寻求系统的积极影响以及扩大不受支持或隐私敏感检索的负面风险。它还描述了预期的发布范围。Justification: The broader-impact and release paragraph discusses the positive impact of lower-cost evidence-seeking systems and the negative risk of amplifying unsupported or privacy-sensitive retrieval. It also describes the intended release scope.

指南：Guidelines:

  • 答案 [N/A] 意味着所做的工作没有社会影响。The answer [N/A] means that there is no societal impact of the work performed.

  • 如果作者回答[不适用]或[否]，他们应该解释为什么他们的工作没有社会影响或为什么论文没有解决社会影响。If the authors answer [N/A] or [No], they should explain why their work has no societal impact or why the paper does not address societal impact.

  • 负面社会影响的例子包括潜在的恶意或非预期用途（例如，虚假信息、生成虚假个人资料、监视）、公平性考虑（例如，部署可能做出不公平地影响特定群体的决策的技术）、隐私考虑和安全考虑。Examples of negative societal impacts include potential malicious or unintended uses (e.g., disinformation, generating fake profiles, surveillance), fairness considerations (e.g., deployment of technologies that could make decisions that unfairly impact specific groups), privacy considerations, and security considerations.

  • 会议预计许多论文将是基础研究，不与特定应用程序相关，更不用说部署了。然而，如果存在任何负面应用的直接路径，作者应该指出。例如，可以合理地指出，生成模型质量的改进可用于生成虚假信息的 Deepfakes。另一方面，无需指出优化神经网络的通用算法可以使人们能够更快地训练生成 Deepfakes 的模型。The conference expects that many papers will be foundational research and not tied to particular applications, let alone deployments. However, if there is a direct path to any negative applications, the authors should point it out. For example, it is legitimate to point out that an improvement in the quality of generative models could be used to generate Deepfakes for disinformation. On the other hand, it is not needed to point out that a generic algorithm for optimizing neural networks could enable people to train models that generate Deepfakes faster.

  • 作者应考虑当技术按预期使用并正常运行时可能出现的危害、当技术按预期使用但给出不正确的结果时可能出现的危害、以及（有意或无意）滥用技术而造成的危害。The authors should consider possible harms that could arise when the technology is being used as intended and functioning correctly, harms that could arise when the technology is being used as intended but gives incorrect results, and harms following from (intentional or unintentional) misuse of the technology.

  • 如果存在负面社会影响，作者还可以讨论可能的缓解策略（例如，模型的门控发布、除了攻击之外还提供防御、监控滥用的机制、监控系统如何随着时间的推移从反馈中学习的机制、提高机器学习的效率和可访问性）。If there are negative societal impacts, the authors could also discuss possible mitigation strategies (e.g., gated release of models, providing defenses in addition to attacks, mechanisms for monitoring misuse, mechanisms to monitor how a system learns from feedback over time, improving the efficiency and accessibility of ML).

11. 保障措施Safeguards

问题：本文是否描述了为负责任地发布滥用风险较高的数据或模型（例如预先训练的语言模型、图像生成器或抓取的数据集）而采取的保障措施？Question: Does the paper describe safeguards that have been put in place for responsible release of data or models that have a high risk for misuse (e.g., pre-trained language models, image generators, or scraped datasets)?

答案：[是]Answer: [Yes]

理由：该文件指出，计划发布的是检索子代理，而不是一般的自主网络代理或答案生成器，并且发布文档将包括预期用途、数据集出处和评估协议。这些就是这里介绍的资产的相关保障措施。Justification: The paper states that the planned release is a retrieval subagent, not a general autonomous web agent or answer generator, and that release documentation will include intended use, dataset provenance, and evaluation protocols. These are the relevant safeguards for the assets introduced here.

指南：Guidelines:

  • 答案 [N/A] 意味着该论文不存在此类风险。The answer [N/A] means that the paper poses no such risks.

61

  • 已发布的具有高误用或双重用途风险的模型应在发布时采取必要的保障措施，以允许模型的受控使用，例如要求用户遵守使用指南或访问模型的限制或实施安全过滤器。Released models that have a high risk for misuse or dual-use should be released with necessary safeguards to allow for controlled use of the model, for example by requiring that users adhere to usage guidelines or restrictions to access the model or implementing safety filters.

  • 从互联网上抓取的数据集可能会带来安全风险。作者应该描述他们如何避免发布不安全的图像。Datasets that have been scraped from the Internet could pose safety risks. The authors should describe how they avoided releasing unsafe images.

  • 我们认识到提供有效的保障措施具有挑战性，许多论文并不要求这样做，但我们鼓励作者考虑到这一点并尽最大努力。We recognize that providing effective safeguards is challenging, and many papers do not require this, but we encourage authors to take this into account and make a best faith effort.

12. 现有资产的许可证Licenses for existing assets

问题：论文中使用的资产（例如代码、数据、模型）的创建者或原始所有者是否得到了适当的认可，许可和使用条款是否明确提及并得到适当尊重？Question: Are the creators or original owners of assets (e.g., code, data, models), used in the paper, properly credited and are the license and terms of use explicitly mentioned and properly respected?

答案：[是]Answer: [Yes]

理由：我们引用了本文中使用的所有现有资产的原始来源，包括基准数据集、模型检查点、检索后端和外部服务。指南：Justification: We cite the original sources for all existing assets used in the paper, including benchmark datasets, model checkpoints, retrieval backends, and external services. Guidelines:

  • 答案 [N/A] 意味着本文不使用现有资产。The answer [N/A] means that the paper does not use existing assets.

  • 作者应该引用生成代码包或数据集的原始论文。The authors should cite the original paper that produced the code package or dataset.

  • 作者应说明使用的是哪个版本的资产，如果可能，还应包含 URL。The authors should state which version of the asset is used and, if possible, include a URL.

  • 每项资产均应包含许可证名称（例如 CC-BY 4.0）。The name of the license (e.g., CC-BY 4.0) should be included for each asset.

  • 对于从特定来源（例如网站）抓取的数据，应提供该来源的版权和服务条款。For scraped data from a particular source (e.g., website), the copyright and terms of service of that source should be provided.

  • 如果发布资产，则应提供包中的许可证、版权信息和使用条款。对于流行的数据集，If assets are released, the license, copyright information, and terms of use in the package should be provided. For popular datasets,paperswithcode.com/datasets已为某些数据集策划了许可证。他们的许可指南可以帮助确定数据集的许可。has curated licenses for some datasets. Their licensing guide can help determine the license of a dataset.

  • 对于重新打包的现有数据集，应提供原始许可证和派生资产的许可证（如果已更改）。For existing datasets that are re-packaged, both the original license and the license of the derived asset (if it has changed) should be provided.

  • 如果无法在线获取此信息，我们鼓励作者联系该资产的创建者。If this information is not available online, the authors are encouraged to reach out to the asset’s creators.

13. 新资产New assets

问题：本文中引入的新资产是否有详细记录，并且文档是否与资产一起提供？Question: Are new assets introduced in the paper well documented and is the documentation provided alongside the assets?

答案：[是]Answer: [Yes]

理由：本文在正文和附录中记录了新的工具、训练配方、提示、奖励设计和评估协议。计划发布的版本将包括权重、代码和生成的训练数据的随附文档。指南：Justification: The paper documents the new harness, training recipe, prompts, reward design, and evaluation protocol in the main text and appendix. The planned release will include accompanying documentation for the weights, code, and generated training data. Guidelines:

  • 答案[N/A]意味着该论文不会发布新资产。The answer [N/A] means that the paper does not release new assets.

  • 研究人员应通过结构化模板将数据集/代码/模型的详细信息作为其提交内容的一部分进行交流。这包括有关培训、许可证、限制等的详细信息。Researchers should communicate the details of the dataset/code/model as part of their submissions via structured templates. This includes details about training, license, limitations, etc.

  • 提交时，请记住对您的资产进行匿名化（如果适用）。您可以创建匿名 URL 或包含匿名 zip 文件。At submission time, remember to anonymize your assets (if applicable). You can either create an anonymized URL or include an anonymized zip file.

16. LLM使用声明Declaration of LLM usage

问题：如果法学硕士是本研究核心方法的重要、原创或非标准组成部分，论文是否描述了法学硕士的用法？请注意，如果法学硕士仅用于写作、编辑或格式化目的并且不Question: Does the paper describe the usage of LLMs if it is an important, original, or non-standard component of the core methods in this research? Note that if the LLM is used only for writing, editing, or formatting purposes and does不not影响研究的核心方法、科学严谨性或原创性，不需要声明。impact the core methodology, scientific rigor, or originality of the research, declaration is not required.

答案：[是]Answer: [Yes]

理由：LLM 是该方法和评估的核心：本文描述了用于 SFT 的教师、经过训练的检索策略、前沿基线、答案生成器和判断提示。正文和附录中提供了相关型号名称和提示。Justification: LLMs are central to the method and evaluation: the paper describes the teacher used for SFT, the trained retrieval policy, frontier baselines, answer generators, and the judge prompt. The relevant model names and prompts are provided in the main text and appendix.

指南：Guidelines:

  • 答案 [N/A] 意味着本研究中的核心方法开发不涉及法学硕士作为任何重要的、原创的或非标准的组成部分。The answer [N/A] means that the core method development in this research does not involve LLMs as any important, original, or non-standard components.

  • 请参阅 NeurIPS 手册中的法学硕士政策，了解应该或不应该描述的内容。Please refer to our LLM policy in the NeurIPS handbook for what should or should not be described.
